# The Wildlife Habitat and Ecological Lands Designation Regulations

> **Unofficial version, for personal and non-commercial use only.** © Office of the King's Printer, Saskatchewan. Not endorsed by or affiliated with the Government of Saskatchewan. Text obtained from the [official source](https://publications.saskatchewan.ca/api/v1/products/70515/formats/78404/download) via the [A2AJ Canadian Laws dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/a2aj/canadian-laws). Always check the official version before relying on it.

RRS c W-13.2 Reg 4

*Made under The Wildlife Habitat Protection Act*

### Title

1 These regulations may be cited as The Wildlife Habitat and Ecological Lands

Designation Regulations.

### Wildlife habitat and ecological lands designated

2 The lands listed in the Appendix are designated as wildlife habitat and ecological

lands pursuant to Section 3 of The Wildlife Habitat Protection Act.

### R.R.S. c.W-13.2 Reg 2 repealed

3 The Treaty Land Entitlement Withdrawal Regulations are repealed.

### R.R.S. c.W-13.2 Reg 3 repealed

4 The Wildlife Habitat Lands Designation Regulations are repealed.

### Coming into force

5(1) Subject to subSection (2), these regulations come into force on the day on

which Section 1 of The Wildlife Habitat Protection (Land Designation) Amendment

Act, 2010 comes into force.

(2) If these regulations are filed with the Registrar of Regulations after the day

on which The Wildlife Habitat Protection (Land Designation) Amendment Act, 2010

comes into force, these regulations come into force on the day on which they are

filed with the Registrar of Regulations.
